## Semantic Chunks
### Section: Introduction
- **Level**: `intro`
- **Hash**: `cd3531b2307bbcddd3f3b9687a7a974323aff0d4348824065f5cc0e1dbfd30e4`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/how-should-schools-document-ai-misconduct-cases` (Similarity: 0.9585)

```text
When escalating an AI misconduct case to an administrator, department chair, or honor council, teachers should provide Checkmark Plagiarism's standardized 4-Part Administrative Case Dossier: 1) Physical Process Telemetry Logs (active typing duration, 0.05-second paste timestamps, deletion ratios, and a secure 15-second writing playback link); 2) Linguistic AI Probability Heatmaps; 3) Citation & DOI Verification Records (proving hallucinated sources via Crossref); and 4) Documented Conference Notes from the 2-minute oral check-in.

Administrators, academic deans, and honor board members operate under strict institutional due process and legal standards. If a teacher forwards a blurry screenshot of an 85% score from an unverified web detector accompanied by a subjective email, the administrator cannot uphold the penalty without exposing the school to appeals or parental challenges. Administrative referrals require structured, tamper-evident, and empirical documentation . Checkmark Plagiarism compiles all physical and forensic evidence into a certified PDF audit dossier with a single click.

Below is a comprehensive guide on preparing defensible, professional AI misconduct documentation for school leadership.

Checkmark Plagiarism prepares administrative case dossiers by pairing essay writing playback with AI detection , plagiarism detection , autograding , and integrations with Canvas and Google Classroom .
```

### Section: The 4 Components of an Administrative Case Dossier
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `1c07a3ce20bf5e17d63be783ce593882b254563947d6d39e6eba24453000f98f`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/how-should-schools-document-ai-misconduct-cases` (Similarity: 0.9004)

```text
1. Keystroke Telemetry & Playback Link

Includes exact active typing minutes (e.g., "8 minutes for 1,600 words"), paste event character counts, and a secure link to the 15-second time-lapse replay.

2. Multi-Signal Linguistic Heatmaps

Displays sentence-by-sentence overlays identifying low-perplexity synthetic prose, formulaic transitions, and deviations from the student's baseline.

3. Citation & DOI Verification Status

Contains database verification records from Crossref confirming that cited academic journal articles or DOIs do not exist in published literature.

4. Structured Educator Conference Notes

Factual, chronological documentation of the date, diagnostic questions asked, student responses, and lack of pre-writing drafting artifacts.
```

### Section: How Certified Dossiers Protect Administrative Due Process
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `a401453289313c5c002d8fb581b959cb4db41743a613f8b4601f1cba87cc927d`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/how-should-schools-document-ai-misconduct-cases` (Similarity: 0.8935)

```text
Understanding why structured documentation ensures institutional defensibility:

Cryptographic Timestamping: Checkmark PDF dossiers are digitally timestamped upon export, proving the evidence was generated directly from LMS telemetry without post-hoc manipulation. 
 Meeting the "Two-Pillar Standard": Administrators can easily verify that the case meets the school handbook's requirement for at least two independent proof categories (telemetry + fake citations). 
 FERPA-Compliant Formatting: All student identifying data is formatted to comply with federal student privacy laws and district data governance policies.

Read more in how Checkmark writing process analysis works .
```

### Section: Comparison: Informal Teacher Emails vs. Checkmark Administrative Dossier
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `3d27ab0c8c314d0158fe281a7cd1537bf771ad343e0c0c75ca0694655f68f23b`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/how-can-teachers-document-an-academic-integrity-investigation` (Similarity: 0.9185)

```text
Checkmark Administrative Dossier (Certified & Defensible)

One-click export from Canvas SpeedGrader. 
 Contains physical keystroke logs and paste timestamps. 
 Automated Crossref DOI verification status. 
 Withstands parent appeals, honor councils, and legal review.

Informal Teacher Emails (Fragile & Subjective)

Subjective email describing the essay as "suspicious." 
 Attaches an unverified screenshot from a free web app. 
 No physical record of typing time or clipboard actions. 
 Vulnerable to immediate administrative reversal on appeal.
```

### Section: A 5-Step Educator Protocol for Administrative Escalation
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `0a7cb05f5b54aab748e388aa4792c39500aba94c57178129a08bb99fd21e6fde`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/how-should-schools-document-ai-misconduct-cases` (Similarity: 0.8156)

```text
Administrative Escalation Checklist:

1. Complete the 2-minute oral check-in with the student; type brief objective notes in Checkmark. 
 2. Click "Export Administrative Dossier (PDF)" inside Canvas SpeedGrader. 
 3. Verify that the dossier satisfies the school's Two-Pillar evidentiary standard. 
 4. Submit the certified PDF dossier to your Academic Dean or Honor Council chair via secure LMS workflow. 
 5. Attend the Honor Board hearing with the compiled physical playback and citation records.
```
